## Section 2: Clinical Conditions Profiles

### Condition: ﻿# Cancer
Category: Metabolic
Associated Products: - diawell
- magilim

Overview:
Cancer is a group of diseases characterized by uncontrolled cell growth and the potential to invade or spread to other parts of the body. It arises from genetic mutations that disrupt normal cellular regulation.

Symptoms:
Symptoms vary by cancer type and location but may include unexplained weight loss, fatigue, pain, skin changes, persistent cough, or abnormal bleeding.

Causes:
Key risk factors include tobacco use, poor diet, excessive alcohol consumption, exposure to carcinogens, infections (e.g., HPV, Hepatitis B/C), and genetic predisposition.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis typically involves imaging (CT, MRI, PET), biopsies, histopathological analysis, and molecular testing to determine cancer subtype and stage.

Treatment:
Treatment options depend on cancer type and stage and may include surgery, radiation therapy, chemotherapy, targeted therapy, immunotherapy, and hormonal therapy.

Prevention:
Preventive measures include smoking cessation, healthy diet rich in fruits and vegetables, regular exercise, vaccination against HPV and Hepatitis B, and screening programs (e.g., mammography, colonoscopy).

### Condition: Hypertension
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: - Cello Q10
- Blood Circulatory Massager

Overview:
Hypertension is a condition in which the force of the blood against the artery walls is too high, often leading to serious health issues.

Symptoms:
Often asymptomatic; may cause headaches, dizziness, shortness of breath, nosebleeds.

Causes:
Causes of Hypertension may include genetic factors, lifestyle, and environmental exposure.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis is established via clinical examination and laboratory findings.

Treatment:
Requires antihypertensive medications as prescribed by a doctor. Regular monitoring of blood pressure. Lifestyle changes such as reducing salt intake and regular exercise.

Prevention:
Prevention strategies involve maintaining a balanced diet, exercise, and routine screenings.

Associated Products: - Cello Q10
- Blood Circulatory Massager

Overview:
Hypertension is a condition in which the force of the blood against the artery walls is too high, often leading to serious health issues.

Symptoms:
Often asymptomatic; may cause headaches, dizziness, shortness of breath, nosebleeds.

Causes:
Causes of Hypertension may include genetic factors, lifestyle, and environmental exposure.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis is established via clinical examination and laboratory findings.

Treatment:
Requires antihypertensive medications as prescribed by a doctor. Regular monitoring of blood pressure. Lifestyle changes such as reducing salt intake and regular exercise.

Prevention:
Prevention strategies involve maintaining a balanced diet, exercise, and routine screenings.

### Condition: ﻿# Cancer
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: General supplements

Overview:
Cancer is a group of diseases characterized by uncontrolled cell growth and the potential to invade or spread to other parts of the body. It arises from genetic mutations that disrupt normal cellular regulation.

Symptoms:
Symptoms vary by cancer type and location but may include unexplained weight loss, fatigue, pain, skin changes, persistent cough, or abnormal bleeding.

Causes:
Key risk factors include tobacco use, poor diet, excessive alcohol consumption, exposure to carcinogens, infections (e.g., HPV, Hepatitis B/C), and genetic predisposition.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis typically involves imaging (CT, MRI, PET), biopsies, histopathological analysis, and molecular testing to determine cancer subtype and stage.

Treatment:
Treatment options depend on cancer type and stage and may include surgery, radiation therapy, chemotherapy, targeted therapy, immunotherapy, and hormonal therapy.

Prevention:
Preventive measures include smoking cessation, healthy diet rich in fruits and vegetables, regular exercise, vaccination against HPV and Hepatitis B, and screening programs (e.g., mammography, colonoscopy).

### Condition: ﻿# Children Health
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: General supplements

Overview:
Children's health encompasses the physical, mental, and social wellâ€‘being of individuals from birth through adolescence. It focuses on growth, development, disease prevention, and the promotion of healthy behaviors.

Symptoms:
Symptoms in children can be nonâ€‘specific; common signs of illness include fever, rash, irritability, poor appetite, fatigue, and developmental delays.

Causes:
Key factors affecting child health include nutrition, immunizations, environmental exposures, genetics, and access to quality healthcare.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosing pediatric conditions often requires ageâ€‘appropriate physical exams, growth charts, laboratory tests, and developmental assessments.

Treatment:
Treatment ranges from vaccination and preventive care to medication, therapy, and surgical interventions, always tailored to the child's age and developmental stage.

Prevention:
Prevention strategies involve immunization schedules, proper nutrition, safe environments, regular screenings, and health education for families.

### Condition: ﻿# Diabetes
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: General supplements

Overview:
Diabetes mellitus is a chronic metabolic disorder characterized by elevated blood glucose levels due to impaired insulin production, insulin resistance, or both. It includes type 1, type 2, gestational, and other specific types.

Symptoms:
Common symptoms are increased thirst, frequent urination, unexplained weight loss, fatigue, blurred vision, and slow wound healing.

Causes:
Type 1 diabetes is autoimmune destruction of beta cells. Type 2 diabetes results from insulin resistance linked to obesity, sedentary lifestyle, genetics, and aging. Gestational diabetes occurs during pregnancy due to hormonal changes.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis is based on fasting plasma glucose, oral glucose tolerance test, HbA1c levels, and sometimes random glucose measurements.

Treatment:
Treatment includes lifestyle modifications, blood glucose monitoring, oral hypoglycemic agents, insulin therapy, and management of complications.

Prevention:
Prevention focuses on maintaining a healthy weight, regular physical activity, balanced diet, and routine screening for atâ€‘risk individuals.

### Condition: ﻿# Heart Disease
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: General supplements

Overview:
Heart disease refers to a range of cardiovascular conditions affecting the heart and blood vessels, including coronary artery disease, heart failure, arrhythmias, and congenital defects.

Symptoms:
Common symptoms include chest pain or discomfort, shortness of breath, fatigue, palpitations, swelling in the legs, and dizziness.

Causes:
Risk factors encompass hypertension, high LDL cholesterol, smoking, diabetes, obesity, sedentary lifestyle, genetic predisposition, and excessive alcohol consumption.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis utilizes electrocardiograms (ECG), echocardiography, stress testing, cardiac catheterization, blood biomarkers (troponin), and imaging such as CT or MRI.

Treatment:
Treatment options range from lifestyle modification and pharmacotherapy (statins, antihypertensives, antiplatelet agents) to surgical interventions like angioplasty, stent placement, coronary artery bypass grafting, and device therapy (pacemakers, defibrillators).

Prevention:
Prevention focuses on controlling blood pressure, maintaining healthy lipids, regular exercise, balanced diet, smoking cessation, and routine health screenings.

Hypertension is a condition in which the force of the blood against the artery walls is too high, often leading to serious health issues.

Symptoms:
Often asymptomatic; may cause headaches, dizziness, shortness of breath, nosebleeds.

Causes:
Causes of Hypertension may include genetic factors, lifestyle, and environmental exposure.

Diagnosis is established via clinical examination and laboratory findings.

Treatment:
Requires antihypertensive medications as prescribed by a doctor. Regular monitoring of blood pressure. Lifestyle changes such as reducing salt intake and regular exercise.

Prevention:
Prevention strategies involve maintaining a balanced diet, exercise, and routine screenings.

### Condition: ﻿# Malaria
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: General supplements

Overview:
Malaria is a lifeâ€‘threatening disease caused by Plasmodium parasites, transmitted to humans through the bite of infected Anopheles mosquitoes. It is prevalent in tropical and subtropical regions.

Symptoms:
Typical symptoms include fever, chills, headache, nausea, vomiting, muscle pain, and, in severe cases, anemia, organ failure, and cerebral involvement.

Causes:
The disease is caused by infection with Plasmodium species (P. falciparum, P. vivax, P. ovale, P. malariae, P. knowlesi). Transmission occurs via mosquito bites, blood transfusion, or congenital transfer.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis is performed using rapid diagnostic tests (RDTs), microscopy of thick and thin blood smears, and polymerase chain reaction (PCR) for species confirmation.

Treatment:
Treatment depends on the Plasmodium species and severity. Firstâ€‘line therapies include artemisininâ€‘based combination therapies (ACTs) for uncomplicated malaria and intravenous artesunate for severe cases.

Prevention:
Prevention strategies encompass vector control (insecticideâ€‘treated nets, indoor residual spraying), prophylactic antimalarial drugs for travelers, and vaccine development (RTS,S/AS01).

### Condition: ﻿# Mental Health
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: General supplements

Overview:
Mental health encompasses emotional, psychological, and social wellâ€‘being, influencing how individuals think, feel, and act throughout life. It affects coping with stress, relationships, and decisionâ€‘making.

Symptoms:
Symptoms of mental health disorders vary widely, ranging from persistent sadness, anxiety, mood swings, irritability, sleep disturbances, to impaired concentration and withdrawal.

Causes:
Factors include genetics, brain chemistry, trauma, chronic stress, substance abuse, and environmental influences such as socioeconomic status and social support.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis is made by mental health professionals using clinical interviews, standardized questionnaires (e.g., PHQâ€‘9, GADâ€‘7), and criteria from DSMâ€‘5 or ICDâ€‘10.

Treatment:
Treatment options involve psychotherapy (CBT, counseling), pharmacotherapy (antidepressants, anxiolytics), lifestyle interventions (exercise, sleep hygiene), and community support.

Prevention:
Prevention focuses on early detection, stress management, fostering supportive environments, promoting mental health education, and reducing stigma.

### Condition: ﻿# Nutrition
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: General supplements

Overview:
Nutrition is the process of providing or obtaining the food necessary for health and growth. It involves the intake, digestion, absorption, metabolism, and utilization of nutrients.

Symptoms:
Nutritional deficiencies can manifest as fatigue, poor wound healing, anemia, weakened immunity, and growth retardation.

Causes:
Causes include inadequate dietary intake, malabsorption disorders, increased physiological needs, and restrictive diets.

Diagnosis:
Assessment involves dietary surveys, anthropometric measurements, blood tests for micronutrients, and metabolic panels.

Treatment:
Treatment includes dietary counseling, supplementation of deficient nutrients, fortification programs, and addressing underlying medical conditions.

Prevention:
Preventive strategies focus on balanced diets rich in fruits, vegetables, whole grains, lean proteins, and healthy fats, as well as public health nutrition education.

### Condition: ﻿# Cancer
Category: Metabolic
Associated Products: - diawell
- magilim

Overview:
Cancer is a group of diseases characterized by uncontrolled cell growth and the potential to invade or spread to other parts of the body. It arises from genetic mutations that disrupt normal cellular regulation.

Symptoms:
Symptoms vary by cancer type and location but may include unexplained weight loss, fatigue, pain, skin changes, persistent cough, or abnormal bleeding.

Causes:
Key risk factors include tobacco use, poor diet, excessive alcohol consumption, exposure to carcinogens, infections (e.g., HPV, Hepatitis B/C), and genetic predisposition.

Diagnosis:
Diagnosis typically involves imaging (CT, MRI, PET), biopsies, histopathological analysis, and molecular testing to determine cancer subtype and stage.

Treatment:
Treatment options depend on cancer type and stage and may include surgery, radiation therapy, chemotherapy, targeted therapy, immunotherapy, and hormonal therapy.

Prevention:
Preventive measures include smoking cessation, healthy diet rich in fruits and vegetables, regular exercise, vaccination against HPV and Hepatitis B, and screening programs (e.g., mammography, colonoscopy).

### Condition: Hypertension
Category: General Wellness
Associated Products: - Cello Q10
- Blood Circulatory Massager

Overview:
Hypertension is a condition in which the force of the blood against the artery walls is too high, often leading to serious health issues.

Symptoms:
Often asymptomatic; may cause headaches, dizziness, shortness of breath, nosebleeds.
